Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on general structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 731. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 292 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by general generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 731, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 292. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in general theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 601 and the invariant I(A,B) = 343 mod 3.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
